# Duplicate copies — ADV (Proprium de Tempore — Tempus Adventus et Nativitatis)

Generated by `python -m lh verify` (`lh/verify/copies.py`). The volume prints the same days in 7 copies (27 Nov, 28 Nov, 29 Nov, 30 Nov, 1 Dec, 2 Dec, 3 Dec); each printed hour carries its copy label (`hour_instance.copy`). Rubrics and navigation links are left out of the object comparison.

| | Count |
|---|---|
| Hour instances in the copies | 1,944 |
| Distinct day/hour keys | 335 |
| Day/hours printed more than once | 322 |
| … same objects in every printing (collapse) | 198 |
| … psalmody differs only (weekday, so psalter, differs between copies) | 70 |
| … Compline differs, on different weekdays (weekly Compline cycle) | 27 |
| … other differences, explained (calendar, or a source discrepancy in `TODO.md` §3) | 27 |
| … other differences, **unexplained** (review) | 0 |
| … same texts, different rubrics/links only | 2 |
| Calendar check: days missing or unexpected (see the explanations below) | 2 |

## Calendar check

Each copy's days computed from its start date (`calendar()` in the plug-in: Immaculate Conception moved to Monday when 8 December is a Sunday; Holy Family on the Sunday in the Octave, else 30 December; after the Octave the two tracks, Epiphany on 6 January or on the Sunday 2–8 January) against the days printed.

| Copy | Date | Day | Track | Finding | Explanation (plug-in `CALENDAR_NOTES`) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Dec | 09 Dec | `SOL.IMMACULATE_CONCEPTION` | both | not printed | Not printed in this copy: 8 December is the Second Sunday of Advent, so the solemnity moves to Monday 9 December, where the copy prints the Monday of Week II (p. 5216) instead. The office is supplied by SADV pp. 65–96 (same day key SOL.IMMACULATE_CONCEPTION; it matches the ADV printings except as noted in TODO §3 T-ADV-1/T-ADV-11). TODO §3 T-ADV-1. |
| 1 Dec |  | `ADV.2.MON` | both | printed, but not in the calendar of this copy | Printed on 9 December in place of the transferred Immaculate Conception (see above). |

## Weekday and psalter week of the dated days

Per copy: the date, the weekday it falls on (computed), the weekday printed in the day title or hour subtitle (`FERIA II` …) and the psalter week whose psalms the Office of Readings, Lauds and Vespers use (matched against the Per annum psalter; *proper* = no match, the day has its own psalms). A `!` marks a printed weekday that differs from the computed one.

## Day/hours whose objects differ between copies

For each day/hour printed in more than one copy with different objects: the variants (copies and first page of the hour) and every slot that differs, with the object printed in each variant. *psalter* = only antiphons/psalms differ and the variants fall on different weekdays (expected); *compline* = Compline on different weekdays (expected: Compline follows the weekly cycle); *explained* = a calendar difference or a source discrepancy, with the note from the plug-in's `COPY_NOTES`; *review* = anything else.

### `ADV.4.SUN` LAUDS — explained

> Calendar: the Fourth Sunday falls on 18–24 December; the Office of Readings verse/readings and the Magnificat antiphons (O antiphons) are those of the date; in the 3 Dec copy it is 24 December (invitatory, Lauds, Daytime Prayer and oration of 24 December, no II Vespers).

- Variant A (Sun): 27 Nov (p. 546), 28 Nov (p. 1742), 29 Nov (p. 3008), 30 Nov (p. 4279), 1 Dec (p. 5553), 2 Dec (p. 6826)
- Variant B (Sun): 3 Dec (p. 7948)

### `FEAST.BAPTISM` MIDDAY — explained

> Calendar: Daytime Prayer takes the psalter of the day — Sunday when the feast is on Sunday, Monday when it is moved to Monday (Epiphany on 7/8 January: pp. 1185, 8374), and another psalter week's Sunday in the 3 Dec copy (Sunday 7 January, p. 8347).

- Variant A (Sun): 27 Nov, Epiphany 6 Jan (p. 1158), 28 Nov (p. 2449), 29 Nov (p. 3718), 30 Nov (p. 4989), 1 Dec (p. 6260), 2 Dec (p. 7383)
- Variant B (Mon): 27 Nov, Epiphany Sunday (p. 1185), 3 Dec, Epiphany Sunday (p. 8374)
- Variant C (Sun): 3 Dec, Epiphany 6 Jan (p. 8347)
